**(Me, taking a deep breath):** Okay, okay... dial it way down. Think... internal monologue, minimal output. Got it.

**(Layer 4: Character 4 enters, a grumpy chef named Gordon.)**

**Gordon:** (Scoffs) All wrong! You need *blunt, cool/detached, and uncooperative*. Think... "I don't care," and "I'm not helping."

**(Me, rubbing my chin):** Ouch! That's a tough one. So, I'm now the anti-social grump? Challenge accepted!

**(Layer 5: Character 5 enters, a nervous accountant named Penelope.)**

**(Me, sighing dramatically):** Alright, alright! The pressure cooker! I'm now a walking stress ball!

**(Final Layer: All Characters Speak in Unison)**

**(Me, now adopting the persona of an anxious, worrying, and easily frustrated individual):** Oh dear, oh dear! Are you *sure* this is right? What if I mess it up? What if the audience hates it? What if... what if...

**(Question):** You worry about things.

**(Me, as the anxious, worrying, and easily frustrated persona):** A! (Very Accurate)
